# Cross-Iteration Integration Rule

**Artifact ID:** `ART-RIR`  
**Normative status:** `ACTIVE_NORMATIVE`  
**Package:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR` · **NON-RELEASE**

## Mandatory invariant

A repair iteration is **not** complete merely because its new artifact is internally coherent.

Before an iteration may be marked complete, every existing normative artifact that consumes, mutates, references, validates, audits, promotes, invalidates, checkpoints, restores, formalizes, or displays the repaired objects must be classified as exactly one of:

1. **Migrated** to the repaired model.  
2. **Explicitly non-normative and quarantined.**  
3. **Scheduled** for a named later iteration with an **active incompatibility warning**.

No legacy artifact may remain silently operational against the repaired model.

## Completion report must include

- affected legacy artifacts;
- their current normative status;
- migration status;
- remaining dialect conflicts;
- temporary compatibility rules;
- prohibited uses;
- responsible later iteration;
- blocker impact;
- evidence that no undocumented compatibility assumption remains.

## Supersession

A new artifact may not silently supersede an old artifact. Supersession must be explicit and machine-trackable in principle (`supersedes_artifact_id`, `supersession_digest` when content-addressed).

## Interim normative precedence (until repair complete)

1. Repair charter / current human repair mandate — highest.  
2. `ART-07b` — canonical mathematical object identity.  
3. `ART-07c` — certificate/bridge typing.  
4. `ART-06b` — authoritative mutation / Control+Research commit semantics.  
5. `ART-04c` — identity, authority, HumanDecision, independence, CERTIFY.  
6. `ART-13b` — PromotionIntent / axis application (when ACTIVE).  
7. `ART-11b` — Audit policy / intent-bound audit (when ACTIVE).  
8. Any legacy artifact conflicting with ART-07b/07c/06b/04c/13b/11b is **non-authoritative** for those scopes.  
9. Legacy artifacts may remain authoritative only for unrepaired concepts.  
10. Future-scheduled legacy artifacts must display an incompatibility warning.  
11. Historical audit/acceptance records = process history only, **not readiness**.  
12. Unresolved conflicts → **fail-closed**.  
13. In conflict, choose fail-closed.

## Artifact normative-status labels

| Label | Meaning |
|-------|---------|
| `ACTIVE_NORMATIVE` | Authoritative for its scope |
| `ACTIVE_PARTIAL` | Authoritative for unrepaired subset; conflicts fail-closed to ART-07b / repair artifacts |
| `QUARANTINED_LEGACY` | Non-authoritative; must not authorize promotion/audit PASS |
| `HISTORICAL_EVIDENCE` | Process history only |
| `SUPERSEDED` | Replaced; do not use |
| `DRAFT_REPAIR` | Under repair review; not frozen |
| `PENDING_MIGRATION` | Still operationally referenced; migration scheduled; unsafe for readiness |

## Fail-closed default

If classification is missing → treat as `QUARANTINED_LEGACY` for readiness and promotion authorization.
# IMPLEMENTATION AND RESEARCH EXECUTION BLOCK

**Status:** **ACTIVE BLOCK**  
**Design version:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR`

## Blocked until human approval

1. Software implementation of the autonomous research system  
2. Autonomous mathematical research cycles (conjecture/proof/Lean execution)  
3. Any claim that the architecture is “final” without `DESIGN_FINAL = approved`  
4. Implementation **planning** treated as authorized while repair iterations incomplete

## Revoked until repair + fresh audit

- Blueprint / implementation-planning readiness  
- `AUDIT-0.3-R20` as clearance  
- C12 credit as blueprint convergence  
- Acceptance PASS labels not bound to a release digest  

See [25-audit-reports/FINAL_AUDIT.md](25-audit-reports/FINAL_AUDIT.md) and [adversarial_review_artifacts/INDEPENDENT_BREAKER_AUDIT.md](adversarial_review_artifacts/INDEPENDENT_BREAKER_AUDIT.md).

## Required human decisions (in order)

1. Complete architecture repair iterations to acceptance conditions  
2. Review frozen release + fresh independent audit evidence  
3. Set `DESIGN_FINAL = approved` in gate log (or request revisions)  
4. Separately approve `IMPLEMENTATION_START` for a build plan phase  
5. Separately approve `RESEARCH_EXECUTION_START` after implementation exists

    72	2. If seeds empty (no citers of `prior`) ⇒ skip mint (`ponytail:`).
    73	3. Else insert DemotionWave `PIN_SUPERSESSION`, `trigger_digest=prior`, seeds = citers of `prior`; `work_items` = `OPEN_CLAIM` for each seed ∪ dependent needing OPEN (PIN does not SUPERSEDE); `cursor=0`; apply I-DW-26.
    74	
    75	**START_DEMOTION_WAVE** (RESEARCH_ORCHESTRATOR) recovery payload:
    76	```text
    77	{ trigger_kind, trigger_digest, seeds[]? }
    78	```
    79	- `FULL_CX`: `trigger_digest=cx_digest`; Commit re-derives seeds = current I-CX-01 hits; caller seeds if present must equal else `SEED_MISMATCH`. Apply I-DW-30 seed SUPERSEDEs for any not yet SUPERSEDED; mint dependent OPEN queue for remaining work; I-DW-26.
    80	- `PIN_SUPERSESSION`: `trigger_digest=prior active_def_version_digest`; seeds = current citers not yet OPEN/SUPERSEDED; mint OPEN queue; I-DW-26.
    81	- `LEAN_GAP`: `trigger_digest` must equal I-DW-32 composite for `(claim, pre, post)` at recovery; seeds={claim}; mint OPEN queue; I-DW-26; no SUPERSEDE.
    82	Existing identical `wave_digest` ⇒ `WAVE_EXISTS` (no cursor reset).
    83	
    84	**ADVANCE_DEMOTION_WAVE** `{wave_digest}`: one `OPEN_CLAIM` at cursor; if current = SUPERSEDED → no-op; else upsert OPEN. cursor += 1. (Floor already broken at mint via I-DW-26.)
    85	
    86	**I-DW-10:** One Commit ⇒ one ADVANCE item.
    87	
    88	**I-DW-32 `LEAN_GAP`:** Minted when DerivedLeanStatus rank drops from ≥4 (FULL/CORE) per ART-10b I-LM-20 / SET_LEAN_TOOLCHAIN.  
    89	`trigger_digest = H("LEAN_GAP", claim_digest, pre_manifest_digest_or_⊥, post_manifest_digest_or_⊥, mint_pre_state_head_digest)`.  
    90	seeds={claim}; work_items=`OPEN_CLAIM` for seed (maturity>OPEN ≠SUPERSEDED) ∪ LOGICAL|DEFINITIONAL dependents with maturity>OPEN ≠SUPERSEDED. No SUPERSEDE. Empty ⇒ skip mint. Apply I-DW-26.
    91	
    92	**I-DW-33 closure expansion:** Fire on any Commit that (a) upserts `ClaimRelation` kind∈{RENAMES,EQUIVALENT_TO}, **or** (b) changes fingerprint-relevant Claim fields used by I-CX-01, **or** (c) upserts a Claim whose `claim_math_fingerprint` equals that of any current I-CX-01 hit of a live FULL CX (new digest joins closure). For each live FULL Counterexample CX, let `H = current I-CX-01 hit set`. Let `covered` = union of seeds of every COMPLETE FULL_CX DemotionWave whose `trigger_digest` is `CX.cx_digest` **or** equals `H("CX_EXPAND", CX.cx_digest, *)` for any mint head. For each `c ∈ H \ covered` that is not SUPERSEDED: DeriveEffects MUST SUPERSEDE `c` and mint a new wave `trigger_kind=FULL_CX`, `trigger_digest=H("CX_EXPAND", CX.cx_digest, mint_pre_state_head_digest)`, seeds=newly hit set (`H \ covered` at this Commit), work_items=OPEN_CLAIM dependents, I-DW-26 (same as I-DW-30 steps 2–5). Empty newly hit set ⇒ no wave.
    93	
    94	---
    95	
    96	## 4. Fences
    97	
    98	**I-DW-20 / I-AP-13:** Any wave with `cursor < len(work_items)` ⇒ non-noop APPLY ⇒ `DEMOTION_WAVE_OPEN`.
    99	
   100	**I-DW-21 / I-AP-06:** If any FULL Counterexample (archived or not) I-CX-01-hits C, non-noop APPLY on C ⇒ `CX_BLOCKS_PROMOTION` unless current maturity is SUPERSEDED **and** ∃ COMPLETE FULL_CX DemotionWave whose seeds contain C and whose `trigger_digest` is either `CX.cx_digest` or an `CX_EXPAND` digest for that CX (`H("CX_EXPAND", CX.cx_digest, *)`).
   101	
   102	**I-DW-22:** SUPERSEDED only via I-DW-30/33/START seed writes (not APPLY).
   103	
   104	**I-DW-23:** SUPERSEDED ⇒ DerivedProofFloor UNPROVED.
   105	
   106	---
   107	## 5. Audit
   108	
   109	ART-11b freshness on maturity upserts + I-CX-01 ∩ EvidenceClosure. I-CX-02 preserved.

     1	# 17b — Checkpoint / Restore Binding (Normative)
     2	
     3	**Artifact ID:** `ART-17b`  
     4	**Version:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR-ITER10.2`  
     5	**Normative status:** `ACTIVE_NORMATIVE`  
     6	**Depends on:** ART-06b · ART-07b · ART-16b · ART-04c · ART-13b · ART-01  
     7	**Package:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR` · **NON-RELEASE**  
     8	**Self-contained:** Sole normative text for durable checkpoint/restore. Closes `B-RECOVERY-PREFIX-01` and `B-HARDSTOP-FENCE-01` restore leg. Supersedes ART-17 / ART-16 P2–P6 restore prose.
     9	
    10	## Purpose
    11	
    12	Restore cannot accept a self-presented event prefix that omits or substitutes later irreversible commits. Validate binds an independent irreversible receipt chain. Hard-stop after restore is ControlState only. Mid-wave crash resume = last CP + forward-fix of durable events through irreversible head (open waves OK; APPLY still fenced).
    13	
    14	`ponytail:` Anchor is a property (append-only monotonic head), not a product store choice. Envelope signing / migration versioning → Iter14. Executable fixtures → Iter12.
    15	
    16	---
    17	
    18	## 1. Objects
    19	
    20	```text
    21	IrreversibleKind                      # exactly one per irreversible Commit
    22	  FULL_CX                             # RECORD_COUNTEREXAMPLE FULL (+ I-DW-30 in same Commit)
    23	  PIN_SUPERSESSION                    # SET_ACTIVE_DEFINITION_HEAD that mints I-DW-31
    24	  LEAN_GAP                            # Commit that mints I-DW-32
    25	  CX_EXPAND                           # Commit that mints I-DW-33
    26	  DEMOTION_START                      # START_DEMOTION_WAVE that inserts a new wave
    27	  HARD_STOP_SET
    28	  HARD_STOP_CLEAR
    29	  EIO_VETO                            # SET_EIO_VETO
    30	
    31	IrreversibleReceipt
    32	  event_seq
    33	  event_digest          # = MutationEvent.event_digest at event_seq
    34	  kind                  # IrreversibleKind
    35	  receipt_digest = H("ART17b.IR.v1", event_seq, event_digest, kind)
    36	
    37	IrreversibleSafetyLog                 # property, not a restorable table
    38	  receipts[]                          # append-only, ordered by event_seq
    39	  irreversible_head_seq               # max event_seq; monotonic
    40	  head_receipt_digest                 # receipt_digest of head
    41	
    42	CheckpointRecord
    43	  checkpoint_id                       # immutable
    44	  event_seq_max                       # inclusive merkle-covered end
    45	  merkle_root
    46	  pin_table_hash
    47	  open_contradiction_ids[]
    48	  gate_status_snapshot                # verify-only; hard_stop = ControlState.HardStopRecord names
    49	  lean_manifest_ids[]
    50	  audit_ledger_tail_id
    51	  irreversible_head_seq_at_create
    52	  irreversible_head_digest_at_create  # head_receipt_digest at create
    53	  release_digest_at_create?           # ART-25b optional at seal-bound ops
    54	  created_at
    55	```
    56	
    57	**I-CP-01:** Checkpoint identity immutable. `gate_status_snapshot.hard_stop` uses ART-06b names: `active`, `source`, `set_at_event_seq`, `signal_ref_digest`, `release_dec_digest` — derived verify field only; **not** restore authority.
    58	
    59	---
    60	
    61	## 2. Irreversible append (I-IR-01)
    62	
    63	At every `I.Commit` accept that matches exactly one `IrreversibleKind` above, append **exactly one** `IrreversibleReceipt` (`event_digest` = that Commit’s `MutationEvent.event_digest`) and advance `irreversible_head_seq` (never decrease). Priority if multiple fire in one Commit: `FULL_CX` > `CX_EXPAND` > `PIN_SUPERSESSION` > `LEAN_GAP` > `DEMOTION_START` > `HARD_STOP_*` > `EIO_VETO` (one receipt; kind = highest priority that fired).
    64	
    65	`ponytail:` Broader human-denial set deferred; upgrade = extend IrreversibleKind only.
    66	
    67	**I-IR-02:** Anchor lives outside the restorable ResearchState store. A candidate event prefix alone is not an authority for the irreversible floor.
    68	
    69	---
    70	
    71	## 3. Merkle (I-CP-10)
    72	
    73	1. Canonicalize each committed `MutationEvent` 0..`event_seq_max` as UTF-8 JSON with sorted keys.  
    74	2. `leaf_i = H(canonical_event_i)`.  
    75	3. Merkle tree pairwise `H(left||right)`; odd promote.  
    76	4. Stored `merkle_root` MUST equal recomputed root over **only** `0..event_seq_max`.
    77	
    78	Legacy ART-17 “`event_seq_max ≥ max_seq(FULL_REFUTE)` from prefix alone” is **non-authoritative**.
    79	
    80	---
    81	
    82	## 4. `I.CheckpointValidate` (I-CP-20)
    83	
    84	**Inputs:** `checkpoint_id`, candidate committed-event prefix covering `0..N` where `N ≥ irreversible_head_seq` and `N ≥ CheckpointRecord.event_seq_max`, full `IrreversibleSafetyLog` receipt chain through current head.
    85	
    86	### Create-time checks (when verifying a just-minted CheckpointRecord)
    87	- `irreversible_head_seq_at_create` = live `irreversible_head_seq`
    88	- `irreversible_head_digest_at_create` = live `head_receipt_digest`
    89	- Merkle over `0..event_seq_max` matches
    90	
    91	### Restore / startup checks (all required; fail-closed)
    92	
    93	| Check | Fail code |
    94	|-------|-----------|
    95	| Recompute merkle over `0..event_seq_max` = stored `merkle_root` | `MERKLE_MISMATCH` |
    96	| Candidate prefix length `N ≥ irreversible_head_seq` | `IRREVERSIBLE_PREFIX` |
    97	| For every receipt with `event_seq ≤ irreversible_head_seq`: prefix event at that seq has `event.event_digest = receipt.event_digest` and maps to `receipt.kind` | `IRREVERSIBLE_PREFIX` |
    98	| Events `event_seq_max+1..N` (if any) are present and contiguous (forward-fix segment) | `FORWARD_FIX_GAP` |
    99	| After replay of `0..N`: `ControlState.hard_stop` equals snapshot hard_stop fields **only if** `N = event_seq_max`; if `N > event_seq_max`, hard_stop MUST equal ControlState derived from full replay (snapshot is tip-at-create verify only) | `HARD_STOP_SNAPSHOT_MISMATCH` |
   100	
   101	**I-CP-22:** Incomplete DemotionWave does **not** fail validate. Mid-wave Create is allowed. APPLY remains fenced by I-DW-20 / I-AP-13.
   102	
   103	Any fail ⇒ route **S15**; **no** `APPLY_PROMOTION` until a later validate PASS.
   104	
   105	**I-CP-21:** Prefer forward-fix (replay CP merkle tip, then events through irreversible head) over backward rollback.
   106	
   107	---
   108	
   109	## 5. Restore (I-CP-30)
   110	
   111	1. Select `CheckpointRecord`; supply durable events `0..N` with `N ≥ max(event_seq_max, irreversible_head_seq)`; run `I.CheckpointValidate`.  
   112	2. On PASS: replay `0..N` under ART-06b; rebuild derived indexes from replayed commits only.  
   113	3. Discard scratch / uncommitted drafts.  
   114	4. If post-restore `ControlState.hard_stop.active=true`: ART-06b I-HS-01 — promotions blocked until `HARD_STOP_CLEAR` + ART-04c `HARD_STOP_RELEASE`. Snapshot cannot clear the stop.  
   115	5. Open waves may be present; ADVANCE continues; APPLY blocked until COMPLETE.
   116	
   117	**I-CP-31:** ART-16 P2/P6 and ART-17 interrupt prose that write `ResearchState.hard_stop` via `I.HardStop` are shadow/non-authoritative. Authoritative path = `HARD_STOP_SET` / `HARD_STOP_CLEAR` → ControlState.
   118	
   119	---
   120	
   121	## 6. Commands
   122	
   123	- `I.CheckpointCreate` — system: mint `CheckpointRecord` at current committed tip (open waves allowed) with live irreversible head seq/digest; merkle over `0..event_seq_max`.  
   124	- `I.CheckpointValidate` — system read-only per I-CP-20.
   125	
   126	No maturity / floor / inference writes.
   127	
   128	---
   129	
   130	## 7. Consumer deltas
   131	
   132	| Artifact | Delta |
   133	|----------|-------|
   134	| ART-07b | CheckpointRecord; IrreversibleReceipt stubs |
   135	| ART-06b | I-IR-01 one receipt per irreversible Commit |
   136	| ART-16b | open-wave restore + Create allowed; APPLY fence unchanged |
   137	| ART-16 / 17 | restore + merkle watermark prose → 17b; hard-stop → ART-06b |
   138	| ART-24 | I.CheckpointValidate trust anchor = this artifact |
   139	| ART-RBL | close B-RECOVERY-PREFIX-01; close B-HARDSTOP-FENCE-01 on freeze |
   140	
   141	---
   142	
   143	## 8. Failures / traces
   144	
   145	`MERKLE_MISMATCH | IRREVERSIBLE_PREFIX | FORWARD_FIX_GAP | HARD_STOP_SNAPSHOT_MISMATCH | CHECKPOINT_UNKNOWN`
   146	
   147	```text
   148	TRACE-10A  present prefix omitting later FULL CX Commit → IRREVERSIBLE_PREFIX
   149	TRACE-10B  restore while hard_stop.active → promote blocked until HARD_STOP_CLEAR
   150	TRACE-10C  crash mid-wave → last CP + forward-fix through demotion mint → PASS; APPLY still DEMOTION_WAVE_OPEN
   151	TRACE-10D  merkle tamper → MERKLE_MISMATCH → S15
   152	TRACE-10E  substitute event at irreversible seq (digest≠receipt) → IRREVERSIBLE_PREFIX
   153	TRACE-10F  CheckpointCreate mid-wave → allowed
   154	```
     1	# 11c — Model / Data Provenance Binding (Normative)
     2	
     3	**Artifact ID:** `ART-11c`  
     4	**Version:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR-ITER11.2`  
     5	**Normative status:** `ACTIVE_NORMATIVE`  
     6	**Depends on:** ART-07b · ART-07c · ART-04c · ART-11b · ART-06b · ART-13b · ART-15 · ART-01  
     7	**Package:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR` · **NON-RELEASE**  
     8	**Self-contained:** Sole normative text for factual DD verification + model-prov lifecycle invalidation.
     9	
    10	## Purpose
    11	
    12	Machine-checkable VERIFIED path for data-independence / FIXED DD. Model provenance replace/revoke invalidates credited acts. ψ data-dependence is Commit-derived.
    13

    17	
    18	## 1. Objects
    19	
    20	```text
    21	DdReadRef
    22	  kind                  # DATASET | CALIBRATION | ADAPTIVE | EXTERNAL_FETCH | OTHER
    23	  digest
    24	
    25	DataDependenceRecord                  # ART-07b identity; derivation_payload MUST include:
    26	  # derivation_payload.reads[] : DdReadRef*   (canonical; absent ⇒ treat as [])
    27

    30	                             verifier_binding_digest)
    31	  dd_digest
    32	  status                                # VERIFIED | REJECTED

    34	  verifier_binding_digest
    35	  intro_event_seq
    36	
    37	CalibrationSubmechanismCert           # minimal typed cert for I-PSI-02
    38	  cal_cert_digest = H("ART11c.CAL.v1", claim_or_mechanism_digest, submechanism_digest,
    39	                      proof_evidence_digest)
    40	  claim_or_mechanism_digest
    41	  submechanism_digest
    42	  proof_evidence_digest                 # ART-07b ProofEvidence; floor ≥ INFORMAL
    43	
    44	ModelProvKey = H(provider_id, model_id) # family key inside ModelProvenanceRecord fields

    47	  model_prov_digest
    48	  at_event_seq

    50	
    51	**I-DDV-01:** `dd_verification_status(dd)` = VERIFIED iff live `DdVerificationRecord` with matching `dd_digest` and `status=VERIFIED`; else not VERIFIED.
    52	
    53	**I-DDV-11:** A VERIFIED `dd_digest` authorizes Claim/certificate C only when `C.parameter_domain_core_digest = D.bound_core_digest`. Else `DD_CORE_MISMATCH` (not authorized even if VERIFIED).
    54	
    55	**I-CERT-DD-01 / `dd_ok`:** Data-independence / FIXED uses require I-DDV-01 VERIFIED **and** I-DDV-11; else `DD_BLOCKED` or `DD_CORE_MISMATCH`.
    56	
    57	---
    58	
    59	## 2. Closure + I-DDV-20 (Commit-executable)
    60	
    61	**Declared closure** of a `DataDependenceRecord` = the `reads[]` array in `derivation_payload` (canonical JSON). No other ambient walk.
    62	
    63	**I-DDV-20** (`REGISTER_DD_VERIFICATION` checks; all required):
    64	1. `dd_digest` resolves to live DataDependenceRecord D.
    65	2. Schema of `D.derivation_schema_id` is registered.
    66	3. Every `reads[].kind` ∈ schema `allowed_read_kinds` (FIXED ⇒ must be empty list and `reads[]` empty).
    67	4. If schema `dd_class_fixed=true`: `reads=[]` else reject `DD_HIDDEN_READ` when any read present; `closure_digest` MUST equal `H("EMPTY")`.
    68	5. If not FIXED: `closure_digest = H(sorted (kind,digest) pairs of reads)` MUST equal payload; empty reads allowed only if schema permits.
    69	6. `status=VERIFIED` only if 1–5 hold; `REJECTED` always allowed for auditor judgment (incl. suspected undeclared reads).
    70	
    71	**Schema marker:** ART-07b `SchemaRegistryEntry` for a FIXED derivation schema MUST set `dd_class_fixed=true` and `allowed_read_kinds=[]` (empty). Non-FIXED schemas list allowed kinds ⊆ {DATASET,CALIBRATION,ADAPTIVE,EXTERNAL_FETCH,OTHER}.
    72	
    73	---

   136	TRACE-11A  FIXED without VERIFIED → DD_BLOCKED

   138	TRACE-11C  requires_DATA_DEP_PSI without HD/cal cert → DATA_DEP_PSI_REQUIRED
   139	TRACE-11D  REGISTER_MODEL_PROV supersede key → MODEL_PROV_STALE on old audit APPLY
   140	TRACE-11E  evidence_ref SUPERSEDED → EVIDENCE_PROV_FAIL
   141	```
     1	# 21b — Conformance Binding (Normative)
     2	
     3	**Artifact ID:** `ART-21b`  
     4	**Version:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR-ITER12.1`  
     5	**Normative status:** `ACTIVE_NORMATIVE`  
     6	**Depends on:** ART-07b · ART-07c · ART-06b · ART-13b · ART-11b · ART-11c · ART-04c · ART-16b · ART-17b · ART-01  
     7	**Package:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR` · **NON-RELEASE**  
     8	**Self-contained:** Sole normative text for hash suite, canonicalization, and executable conformance fixtures. Closes ART-07b §1.2 algorithm deferral. Supersedes ART-21 T-suite for repair-package checks only.
     9	
    10	## Purpose
    11	
    12	Pin `H` + canonical serialization so digests are interoperable. Bind a minimal fixture catalog with locked golden digests and concrete command sequences. Executable harness is a **design interpreter** for fixtures — not research execution, not blueprint clearance.
    13	
    14	`ponytail:` Full Q02–Q17 prompt evaluators and migration replay suites remain optional expansions. Release-bound digests = ART-25b. This artifact’s PASS ≠ `DESIGN_FINAL`.
    15	
    16	---
    17	
    18	## 1. Hash suite
    19	
    20	**I-H-01:** `H(bytes) = SHA-256(bytes)` → 32-byte digest; hex lowercase UTF-8 when embedded in JSON.  
    21	**I-H-02:** Normative `H(...)` in this package means I-H-01 over the multi-arg packing in I-H-03, unless the call site says `H(canonical_serialization(obj))` (then I-CAN-02).  
    22	**I-H-03 Multi-arg packing:** `H(a0, a1, …, an)` = `SHA-256(canonical_serialization([a0,…,an]))` where the list is a JSON array; nested objects/arrays use I-CAN-01; byte strings appear as hex lowercase JSON strings when they are digests.  
    23	**I-H-04:** `canonicalization_version = "ART21b.CANON.v1"`.
    24	
    25	---
    26	
    27	## 2. Canonical serialization (I-CAN-01)
    28	
    29	`canonical_serialization(obj)` → UTF-8 bytes of JSON:
    30	
    31	1. Object keys sorted lexicographically (UTF-8 byte order).  
    32	2. Arrays preserve specified normative order; when a rule says “sorted”, sort by canonical JSON of elements.  
    33	3. Absent optional field ⇒ key omitted (never `null`).  
    34	4. No `null` values; use omission or typed token `"⊥"` only where a schema allows it.  
    35	5. Numbers: integers as JSON numbers without exponent; forbid floats in normative fields.  
    36	6. Strings: UTF-8; escape per RFC 8259.  
    37	7. Compact JSON (no insignificant whitespace).
    38	
    39	**I-CAN-02 Top-level object digest:** `SHA-256( UTF-8("ART21b.CANON.v1") || 0x00 || canonical_serialization(obj) )`.  
    40	Domain-tagged digests that begin with a literal tag via I-H-03 (`H("ART16b.DW.v4", …)`) do **not** also apply I-CAN-02.
    41	
    42	**I-CAN-03:** Unknown keys in a normative payload ⇒ `UNKNOWN_FIELD`.
    43	
    44	---
    45	
    46	## 3. Golden digest vectors (I-GV-01)
    47	
    48	Authoritative bodies: `21-acceptance-tests/fixtures/GV-*.json` with `input` + `expected_digest_hex` locked under I-H-01..04 / I-CAN-01..02. Recompute MUST match. Mismatch ⇒ `DIGEST_MISMATCH`. First-run lock forbidden after freeze.
    49	
    50	| Vector ID | Digest rule |
    51	|-----------|-------------|
    52	| GV-CLAIM, GV-PI, GV-AUDIT, GV-CMD, GV-DDV, GV-CP | I-CAN-02 over `input` |
    53	| GV-DW | I-H-03 `H("ART16b.DW.v4", trigger_kind, trigger_digest, seeds, work_items)` from `input` |
    54	
    55	---
    56	
    57	## 4. Harness interface (I-CF-01)
    58	
    59	```text
    60	I.ConformanceRun
    61	  input:  fixture_id, package_manifest_digest
    62	  output: { pass: bool, code?, observed_digest?, expected_digest? }
    63	```
    64	
    65	**I-CF-02:** Ephemeral in-memory replay only; MUST NOT write durable research stores or clear `IMPLEMENTATION_BLOCK`.  
    66	**I-CF-03:** `package_manifest_digest = H` (I-H-03) over sorted `(path, content_digest)` pairs of ACTIVE_NORMATIVE repair artifacts in ART-ASI.  
    67	**I-CF-04:** Catalog PASS ⇒ `CONFORMANCE_CATALOG_PASS` for that manifest only — **not** `DESIGN_FINAL` / blueprint / release.
    68	
    69	**Harness-local ops** (not ResearchState command_kinds): `EVAL_BRIDGE`, `EVAL_CERT_USE`, `I.CheckpointValidate` — pure evaluators over fixture state. All other `command_kind` values MUST be ART-06b / consumer Commit kinds.
    70	
    71	---
    72	
    73	## 5. Fixture catalog
    74	
    75	Authoritative bodies under `21-acceptance-tests/fixtures/CF-*.json`. Each MUST include `initial_state`, `commands[]`, and `expect` or `expect_sequence`.
    76	
    77	| ID | Expect |
    78	|----|--------|
    79	| CF-3A | SUCCESS |
    80	| CF-3B | CALLER_BOOLEAN_FORBIDDEN |
    81	| CF-3C | SUCCESS (+ hard_stop active) |
    82	| CF-3D | STALE_WRITE |
    83	| CF-3F | REJECT |
    84	| CF-3G | HARD_STOP_ACTIVE |
    85	| CF-F2-OK | SUCCESS |
    86	| CF-F2-02 | TYPED_REJECT |
    87	| CF-F2-09 | PROOF_FLOOR_INSUFFICIENT |
    88	| CF-Q01 | SUCCESS |
    89	| CF-Q11 | SUCCESS |
    90	| CF-AUDIT-CARD | UNKNOWN_AUDIT_QUESTION |
    91	| CF-DDV-OK | VERIFIED |
    92	| CF-DD-CORE | DD_CORE_MISMATCH |
    93	| CF-MP-STALE | MODEL_PROV_STALE |
    94	| CF-7A | SUCCESS (seed SUPERSEDED) |
    95	| CF-10A | IRREVERSIBLE_PREFIX |
    96	| CF-10C | SUCCESS then DEMOTION_WAVE_OPEN |
    97	| CF-AP-BOOL | CALLER_BOOLEAN_FORBIDDEN |
    98	| CF-AP-CX | CX_BLOCKS_PROMOTION |
    99	
   100	**I-CF-10:** Missing/empty `commands` or missing golden `expected_digest_hex` ⇒ `FIXTURE_MISSING`.
   101	
   102	---
   103	
   104	## 6. ART-21 supersession
   105	
   106	ART-21 T01–T24 remain HISTORICAL_EVIDENCE. Repair conformance = this catalog. **Release rebinding** = ART-25b (`release_digest` / I-REL-11); seal-bound conformance MUST use fixture digests equal to the sealed `fixture_root_digest` / `conformance_catalog_digest`.

    47	## 2. `I.RoleCeiling` (I-OM-10)
    48	
    49	**day1_profile_digest (I-OM-08):**  
    50	`day1_profile_digest = H("ART04d.DAY1.v1", sorted day-1 role_ids, sorted conditional role_ids)`.  
    51	Live ceiling profile = `ControlState.role_ceiling_profile_digest` (ART-06b). At package genesis / IDENTITY_ADMIN Commit, that field MUST equal `day1_profile_digest` unless a `ROLE_EXPANSION` chain has installed a superseding profile digest (logged). Harness/default: equal to I-OM-08.
    52	
    53	**Derived `roles_invoked` (I-OM-09):** At Commit validate, `roles_invoked = { RoleBinding.role_id of every principal that authorizes this command }`:
    54	1. Always include the authenticated caller’s live binding `role_id`.  
    55	2. Include every additional principal named in the command payload whose RoleBinding is required for success (e.g. certifier on ATTACH_CERTIFICATION, auditor on RECORD_AUDIT).  
    56	3. Never accept a caller-supplied `roles_invoked[]` array (ignore if present).
    57	
    58	**I.RoleCeiling:** Pass iff all hold:
    59	1. Each role in `roles_invoked` is either (a) day-1, or (b) conditional with I-OM-02 trigger satisfied, or (c) covered by live EffectiveDecision(`ROLE_EXPANSION`, `H(day1_profile_digest, role_id)`) approve unexpired at Commit seq.  
    60	2. Each included principal has a live ART-04c RoleBinding at Commit seq.  
    61	3. On certify/propose pairs: PROOF_PROPOSER ≠ PROOF_CERTIFIER principals (I-RB-02).  
    62	4. `command_kind=LOCK_CYCLE` ⇒ FRONTIER_SCHEDULER ∈ roles_invoked.
    63	
    64	Fail ⇒ `ROLE_CEILING`.
    65	
    66	**I-OM-11:** Major APPLY ValidationPreimage `roles_invoked_ok` ⇔ I-OM-10 PASS for that Commit (Commit-derived only).

     1	# 25b — Release Identity Binding (Normative)
     2	
     3	**Artifact ID:** `ART-25b`  
     4	**Version:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR-ITER14.1`  
     5	**Normative status:** `ACTIVE_NORMATIVE`  
     6	**Depends on:** ART-21b · ART-ASI · ART-25 · ART-17b · ART-01 · ART-RBL  
     7	**Package:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR` · **NON-RELEASE** until ReleaseManifest sealed  
     8	**Self-contained:** Sole normative text for immutable release identity. Targets `B-RELEASE-IDENTITY-01` and `B-RELEASE-FALSEPASS-01`.
     9	
    10	## Purpose
    11	
    12	One content-addressed `ReleaseManifest` binds the package. Historical ART-21 / R20 PASS cannot imply readiness. Fresh audit + Sol package gate bind to the manifest digest — not to repair-phase pins alone.
    13	
    14	`ponytail:` Sealing the manifest does not approve `DESIGN_FINAL`; that remains HUMAN. Envelope product PKI deferred; property = digest binding.
    15	
    16	---
    17	
    18	## 1. ReleaseManifest (I-REL-01)
    19	
    20	```text
    21	ReleaseManifest
    22	  release_id                          # immutable label, e.g. ARCH-0.3-REPAIR-CANDIDATE
    23	  package_version                     # single version string for the sealed set
    24	  canonicalization_version            # MUST = ART21b.CANON.v1
    25	  artifact_entries[]                  # sorted by path
    26	    path                              # repo-relative
    27	    artifact_id
    28	    normative_status                  # ACTIVE_NORMATIVE | … at seal time
    29	    content_digest                    # SHA-256 of file bytes (raw), hex lowercase
    30	  fixture_root_digest                 # H over sorted fixture path+content digests (ART-21b)
    31	  conformance_catalog_digest          # digest of ART-21b catalog id list + expected codes
    32	  blocker_ledger_digest               # content_digest of BLOCKER_LEDGER.md at seal
    33	  irreversible_policy_digest          # ART-17b IrreversibleKind enum digest
    34	  sealed_at                           # ISO-8601 UTC string
    35	  prior_release_digest?               # ⊥ for first candidate
    36	
    37	release_digest = H("ART25b.REL.v1", release_id, package_version, canonicalization_version,
    38	                   artifact_entries_canonical, fixture_root_digest, conformance_catalog_digest,
    39	                   blocker_ledger_digest, irreversible_policy_digest, sealed_at,
    40	                   prior_release_digest_or_⊥)
    41	```
    42	
    43	**I-REL-02:** Identity immutable. Any byte change of a listed path ⇒ new manifest / new `release_digest`.  
    44	**I-REL-03:** At most one **sealed** candidate per `release_id`. Supersession uses new `release_id` + `prior_release_digest`.
    45	
    46	---
    47	
    48	## 2. Seal / false-pass controls (I-REL-10)
    49	
    50	**SEAL_RELEASE_MANIFEST** (IDENTITY_ADMIN or designated RELEASE_ADMIN): Commit upserts ReleaseManifest; computes digests under ART-21b. Package remains `NON_RELEASE` until HUMAN `DESIGN_FINAL` targets this `release_digest`.
    51	
    52	**I-REL-11 False-pass ban:** The following MUST NOT be cited as current readiness or as substitutes for a seal bound to `release_digest`:
    53	- ART-21 T01–T24 historical PASS  
    54	- `AUDIT-0.3-R20` / C12 under R20  
    55	- Any `CONFORMANCE_CATALOG_PASS` bound only to a repair `package_manifest_digest` without equality to this seal’s fixture/conformance digests  
    56	- Repair-phase ART-25 pins without `release_digest`
    57	
    58	**I-REL-12:** ART-25 current posture fields that claim clearance MUST include `bound_release_digest = release_digest` or remain explicitly non-clearance (`NO CURRENT AUDIT PASS`).
    59	
    60	---
    61	
    62	## 3. Dual-dialect residual (I-REL-20)
    63	
    64	**B-OBJ-DUAL-01 at seal:** Every ACTIVE_NORMATIVE artifact entry MUST be digest-native at its Commit boundary (ART-21b / ART-07b). Remaining descriptive ID-native prose in PENDING_MIGRATION / appendix files is allowed only with an INCOMPATIBILITY WARNING pointing to the digest-native authority. Full prose purge is not required for seal; residual dual in appendix ≠ open blocker if ACTIVE paths are digest-only.
    65	
    66	**I-REL-21:** Checkpoint envelopes at seal cite `release_digest` in create metadata when ART-17b CheckpointRecord is extended: optional field `release_digest_at_create` (Iter14). Unsigned envelopes remain valid for repair; seal-bound ops SHOULD include the field.
    67	
    68	---
    69	
    70	## 4. Fresh audit binding (I-REL-30)
    71	
    72	A **fresh package audit** for readiness evidence MUST:
    73	1. Name `release_digest` under review.  
    74	2. Run ART-21b catalog against that seal’s fixture digests.  
    75	3. Record Sol (or successor) A–D combined verdict bound to `release_digest`.  
    76	4. Not treat pre-seal internal FREEZE_OK alone as package audit PASS.
    77	
    78	**I-REL-31:** `DESIGN_FINAL` HumanDecision `target_digest` MUST equal `release_digest`. Else gate fail.
    79

     1	# 06b — Authoritative State and Mutation Semantics (Normative)
     2	
     3	**Artifact ID:** `ART-06b`  
     4	**Version:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR-ITER3.2`  
     5	**Normative status:** `ACTIVE_NORMATIVE` (Iteration 3 complete; Sol reviews A–D PASS on ITER3.2)  
     6	**Depends on:** `ART-07b` ITER1.26 · `ART-07c` ITER2.7  
     7	**Supersedes for mutation/commit authority:** ART-06 event/registry poke prose; ART-24 direct `I.HardStop` / ProposeWrite-alone  
     8	**Authority:** Design material. Changes reset Iteration-3 readiness.
     9	
    10	## Purpose
    11	
    12	One logically serialized authoritative mutation boundary. No caller-trusted booleans. ControlState fenced from ResearchState. Hard-stop is a commit-linearized fence. Stale writes fail closed. Event log is replay-sufficient.
    13	
    14	## Scope lock
    15	
    16	Architecture properties only — not a DB/queue/consensus product. Serialization = total order of accepted commits for Control+Research+Design.
    17	
    18	---
    19	
    20	## 1. State stores (three)
    21	
    22	| Store | Contents | Mutated only via |
    23	|-------|----------|------------------|
    24	| **ControlState** | Exact record below | `I.Commit` |
    25	| **ResearchState** | ART-07b/07c objects + side tables + **append-only MutationEvent log** | `I.Commit` |
    26	| **DesignState** | Architecture package artifacts / critique ledger | `I.Commit` (`store_targets` includes DESIGN) |
    27	
    28	```text
    29	ControlState                          # exact field set — no ellipsis
    30	  event_seq                           # u64; last accepted commit seq (0 if none)
    31	  hard_stop                           # HardStopRecord
    32	  role_ceiling_profile_digest         # digest of active OPERABLE_MINIMAL / expanded profile
    33	```
    34	
    35	```text
    36	HardStopRecord
    37	  active                              # bool
    38	  source                              # HUMAN | BUDGET | SYSTEM | ⊥ if !active
    39	  set_at_event_seq                    # 0 if !active
    40	  signal_ref_digest                   # ⊥ if !active
    41	  release_dec_digest                  # ⊥ if active or never cleared; set only on CLEAR
    42	```
    43	
    44	**I-STATE-SEP-01:** Research object identity and promotion/audit effects live only in ResearchState (plus the MutationEvent log stored there). ControlState never stores Claims/certs/bridges as research authority.  
    45	**I-STATE-SEP-02:** A single commit may list multiple `store_targets`, but effects for each store are typed and validated; no silent dual-write from agent scratch.
    46	
    47	---
    48	
    49	## 2. Heads (closed digests)
    50	
    51	```text
    52	control_head_digest = H(
    53	  event_seq,
    54	  hard_stop.active, hard_stop.source, hard_stop.set_at_event_seq,
    55	  hard_stop.signal_ref_digest, hard_stop.release_dec_digest,
    56	  role_ceiling_profile_digest
    57	)
    58	
    59	research_snapshot_digest = H(canonical serialization of all ResearchState
    60	  objects/side-tables EXCLUDING the MutationEvent log)
    61	
    62	research_head_digest = H(event_seq, research_snapshot_digest,
    63	  H(ordered MutationEvent.event_digest for seq=1..event_seq))
    64	
    65	design_head_digest = H(event_seq, DesignState snapshot digest)
    66	
    67	state_head_digest = H(event_seq, control_head_digest, research_head_digest, design_head_digest)
    68	```
    69	
    70	**I-HEAD-01:** Heads are DERIVED only. Agent-supplied head values are never authoritative.
    71	
    72	---
    73	
    74	## 3. Command and effects (bound)
    75	
    76	```text
    77	Command                               # sole preimage; digest computed inside I.Commit
    78	  command_kind
    79	  store_targets[]                     # nonempty subset of {CONTROL, RESEARCH, DESIGN}
    80	  payload                             # typed per command_kind
    81	  expected_state_head_digest          # = H(event_seq, control, research, design heads)
    82	  caller_principal_digest             # ART-04c — required when ART-04c ACTIVE
    83	  caller_binding_digest
    84	  # caller_signature_material NOT in command_digest preimage for unsigned hash;
    85	  # signature verifies over H(Command without signature field)
    86	
    87	command_digest = H(Command without caller_signature_material)
    88	
    89	Effects                               # DERIVED — never caller-authoritative
    90	  effects = DeriveEffects(Command, pre_ControlState, pre_ResearchState, pre_DesignState)
    91	  effects_digest = H(Effects)
    92	```
    93	
    94	**I-EFF-01:** `I.Commit` MUST set `effects = DeriveEffects(...)`. Validator validates **Command** and the derived **Effects**. No alternate effect set may be appended.  
    95	**I-EFF-02:** `MutationEvent` stores the full `Effects` envelope (not only its digest) so the log is replay-sufficient.
    96	
    97	```text
    98	MutationEvent                         # append-only in ResearchState log
    99	  event_seq
   100	  command                             # full Command preimage
   101	  effects                             # full Effects envelope
   102	  validation_preimage                 # derived predicates, reason_codes, art07c_results[]
   103	  event_digest = H(event_seq, command_digest, effects_digest,
   104	                   H(validation_preimage), pre_state_head_digest)
   105	```
   106	
   107	---
   108	
   109	## 4. Mutation boundary (exclusive)
   110	
   111	### I.Commit  (**sole mutator**; sole public write interface)
   112	
   113	**In:** `Command` (inline).  
   114	**Out:** `ACCEPTED{event_seq, event_digest, new_state_head_digest} | REJECTED{reason_codes[]}`.
   115	
   116	**Pipeline (one indivisible transition; logical single-committer exclusivity — no `commit_busy` state bit):**
   117	1. Recompute `state_head_digest`; if ≠ `Command.expected_state_head_digest` → `STALE_WRITE`.  
   118	2. **ART-04c I-CMD-AUTH-01** (when ART-04c ACTIVE): authenticate caller + authorize command_kind.  
   119	3. Hard-stop fence (§5) on **derived effects**.  
   120	4. Caller-boolean ban (§6) on Command.payload.  
   121	5. `effects = DeriveEffects(...)`; validate Command+Effects (ART-07b/07c/04c as applicable).  
   122	6. **Tentative** post-state = Reduce(pre, effects). If reduce would fail → `REDUCE_ABORT` (**no** event appended).  
   123	7. Else atomically: append MutationEvent; install post-state; `event_seq+=1`; recompute heads; ACCEPTED.
   124	
   125	**I-MUT-01:** No normative write outside `I.Commit`.  
   126	**I-MUT-02:** Rejected commits append nothing and leave heads unchanged (ART-24).  
   127	**I-MUT-03:** Direct registry poke / scratch-as-authority → invalid.
   128	
   129	`DeriveEffects` / `Reduce` are **internal pure functions** of Commit — not separate public interfaces.
   130	
   131	---
   132	
   133	## 5. Hard-stop fencing
   134	
   135	**I-HS-01:** While `hard_stop.active=true`, Commit REJECTS unless **all** derived effects are in the allowlist:
   136	- ControlState-only updates to `HardStopRecord` / `role_ceiling_profile_digest` / ControlState decision-log append for `HARD_STOP_RELEASE` only  
   137	- command kinds `HARD_STOP_SET`, `HARD_STOP_CLEAR`, `REGISTER_DD_VERIFICATION`, `REVOKE_MODEL_PROV`, `RECORD_CAL_SUBMECH_CERT`, `CONTROL_DIAGNOSTIC_NOOP`, and `RECORD_HUMAN_DECISION` **only when** `gate_id=HARD_STOP_RELEASE`  
   138	with `store_targets = {CONTROL}` and **zero** ResearchState and **zero** DesignState mutations  
   139	
   140	`HARD_STOP_CLEAR` payload MAY embed the HumanDecision envelope (preferred during stop) so release need not be a prior ResearchState write.
   141	
   142	**I-HS-02:** `HARD_STOP_SET` / `HARD_STOP_CLEAR` transitions:
   143	
   144	| Kind | Requires | Post HardStopRecord |
   145	|------|----------|---------------------|
   146	| SET | `payload.source ∈ {HUMAN,BUDGET,SYSTEM}`, `signal_ref_digest` | `active=true`, source/signal/set_at_event_seq set, **`release_dec_digest=⊥`** |
   147	| CLEAR | `payload.release_dec_digest ≠ ⊥` | `active=false`; `release_dec_digest=payload.release_dec_digest`; source/signal/set_at = ⊥ / 0 |
   148	
   149	**I-HS-03:** Fence check uses ControlState fixed after stale-write head agreement and before accept — linearizable in the commit total order.  
   150	**I-HS-04:** `HARD_STOP_CLEAR` requires `release_dec_digest` → ART-04c-valid HumanDecision `HARD_STOP_RELEASE` whose `target_digest` binds this freeze (`H(set_at_event_seq, signal_ref_digest)`), when ART-04c is ACTIVE.  
   151	**I-HS-05:** Legacy `ResearchState.hard_stop` / direct `I.HardStop` are non-authoritative.
   152	
   153	---
   154	
   155	## 6. Caller-boolean ban
   156	
   157	Forbidden as authoritative payload fields (reject `CALLER_BOOLEAN_FORBIDDEN`):
   158	
   159	```text
   160	dep_closure_ok | contradiction_clear | eio_pass | lit_closure_ok
   161	hop_chain_ok | lean_manifest_ok | audit_pass | bridge_ok | cert_ok
   162	math_stable | roles_ok | floor_ok | applicable | any authorizing *_ok
   163	```
   164	
   165	**I-BOOL-01:** Derived predicates appear only inside `validation_preimage`.  
   166	**I-BOOL-02:** Commands that would **apply** research-maturity / inference-applicability **axis** transitions → `AXIS_WRITE_FORBIDDEN`, except `APPLY_PROMOTION` under ART-13b; `ADVANCE_DEMOTION_WAVE`; and DeriveEffects seed-`SUPERSEDED` writes of `RECORD_COUNTEREXAMPLE` (FULL) / `START_DEMOTION_WAVE` / I-DW-33 under ART-16b. Legacy codeword `PROMOTION_DEFERRED_ITER5` is an alias of `AXIS_WRITE_FORBIDDEN` for other non-APPLY commands until consumers migrate.  
   167	Exception (ART-04c): `ATTACH_CERTIFICATION` may add CertificationRecord / proof-attachment links that make `DerivedProofFloor` evaluate to CERTIFIED_INFORMAL; it MUST NOT write ResearchMaturityRecord or inference axis fields.  
   168	**ART-16b DeriveEffects:** I-DW-30/31/32/33/START mint waves + I-DW-26 `DemotionFloorBreak` upserts are Commit-derived (not caller booleans).
   169	
   170	---
   171	
   172	## 7. ResearchState keys
   173	
   174	Authoritative keys = ART-07b/07c digests (`claim_digest`, `cx_digest`, …).  
   175	**I-REG-01:** Legacy IDs alias-only if 1:1 bound; else `UNTYPED_LEGACY_OBJECT`.  
   176	**I-REG-02:** ID-keyed cert/bridge tables non-authoritative.
   177	
   178	---
   179	
   180	## 8. Failure taxonomy
   181	
   182	```text
   183	STALE_WRITE | HARD_STOP_ACTIVE | CALLER_BOOLEAN_FORBIDDEN
   184	PROMOTION_DEFERRED_ITER5 | AXIS_WRITE_FORBIDDEN | UNKNOWN_COMMAND | INVALID_PAYLOAD
   185	DIGEST_MISMATCH | UNTYPED_LEGACY_OBJECT | STORE_SEPARATOR_VIOLATION
   186	ART07_REJECT | ART07C_REJECT | ROLE_CEILING_REJECT | REDUCE_ABORT
   187	EFFECTS_MISBIND
   188	AUDIT_REQUIRED | AUDIT_FAIL | AUDIT_STALE | AUDIT_INDEPENDENCE | AUDIT_EVIDENCE_MISSING | DISCONFIRM_MISSING | UNKNOWN_AUDIT_QUESTION
   189	```
   190	
   191	**Command kinds (additive; auth ART-04c / ART-13b / ART-11b / ART-16b / ART-10b / ART-08d):** includes `APPLY_PROMOTION`, `RECORD_EIO_ASSESSMENT`, `SET_EIO_VETO`, `CLEAR_EIO_VETO`, `RECORD_AUDIT`, `RECORD_DISCONFIRM`, `RECORD_COUNTEREXAMPLE`, `SET_ACTIVE_DEFINITION_HEAD`, `ATTACH_CERTIFICATION`, `START_DEMOTION_WAVE`, `ADVANCE_DEMOTION_WAVE`, `RECORD_LEAN_MANIFEST`, `SET_LEAN_TOOLCHAIN`, `LOCK_CYCLE`, `BIND_CYCLE_CARD`, `RECORD_CYCLE_ATTACK_LOG`, `RECORD_CYCLE_AUDIT`, `RECORD_CYCLE_LEAN`, `ADVANCE_CYCLE`, `CLOSE_CYCLE`, `HARD_STOP_SET`, `HARD_STOP_CLEAR`, `REGISTER_DD_VERIFICATION`, `REVOKE_MODEL_PROV`, `RECORD_CAL_SUBMECH_CERT`, …  
   192	**ART-16b DeriveEffects:** `RECORD_COUNTEREXAMPLE` (FULL) and `SET_ACTIVE_DEFINITION_HEAD` (head replace) MUST mint DemotionWave at cursor 0 in the same Commit (I-DW-30/31). I-DW-33 expansion and I-DW-32 LEAN_GAP mint under the same Commit when their triggers fire. I-DW-26 floor breaks on every mint.  
   193	**ART-10b DeriveEffects:** `RECORD_LEAN_MANIFEST` / `SET_LEAN_TOOLCHAIN` mint LEAN_GAP per I-LM-20 / I-DW-32 when status drops.  
   194	**ART-08d:** cycle commands mutate CycleRecord / QuarantineLock only via DeriveEffects above.
   195

   200	TRACE-3A sole write path · 3B caller boolean · 3C hard-stop (CONTROL-only effects) · 3D stale `state_head` · 3E promotion deferred · 3F unbound legacy id · 3G HARD_STOP_* with Research effect → HARD_STOP_ACTIVE · 3H validate≠effects attempt impossible under I-EFF-01.

   210	## 11. Alignment / non-goals
   211	
   212	ART-06 quarantined. ART-24 Commit-only. PromotionIntent=ART-13b. Identity=ART-04c. Demotion=Iter7. Restore anchor=Iter10.
    80	Missing, unbound, or mismatched ⇒ no credit (not a credited act).  
    81	**I-MP-20:** Material replace/revoke of live `model_prov_digest` D ⇒ ART-11c ModelProvInvalidation; prior credited acts pinned to D are stale for APPLY (ART-11c).

    84	
    85	## 4. RoleBinding
    86	
    87	```text
    88	RoleBinding
    89	  binding_digest                      # = H(principal_digest, role_id, model_prov_digest_or_⊥,
    90	                                      #    valid_from_event_seq, valid_to_event_seq_or_⊥)
    91	  principal_digest
    92	  role_id                             # PROOF_PROPOSER | PROOF_CERTIFIER | EIO | INTEGRATION_AUDITOR
    93	                                      # | RESEARCH_ORCHESTRATOR | FRONTIER_SCHEDULER | LEAN_VERIFIER
    94	                                      # | HUMAN_GATE_OPERATOR | COMMITTER | IDENTITY_ADMIN
    95	                                      # | LITERATURE_ANALYST | RESEARCH_SCOPE | COUNTEREXAMPLE_ATTACKER  # ART-04d
    96	                                      # | …
    97	  model_prov_digest?                  # required iff principal.kind=MODEL_RUNTIME
    98	  valid_from_event_seq
    99	  valid_to_event_seq?
   100	```
   101	
   102	**I-RB-01:** Live = `valid_from ≤ event_seq` and (`valid_to` absent or `event_seq < valid_to`) and principal not revoked.  
   103	**I-RB-02:** Overlapping live PROOF_PROPOSER + PROOF_CERTIFIER for same principal → invalid.  
   104	**I-RB-03:** BIND_ROLE for HUMAN_GATE_OPERATOR / IDENTITY_ADMIN / COMMITTER requires caller authorized by TrustRoot policy (typically genesis or IDENTITY_ADMIN).
   105	
   106	Independence is **not** a field on RoleBinding — derived in §5.
   107	
   108	---
   109	
   110	## 5. Independence (authoritative closure)
   111	
   112	```text
   113	IndependenceAtom
   114	  atom_digest                         # = H(policy_digest, controller_key_digest,
   115	                                      #    controller_attestation_digest, member_principal_digests_sorted)
   116	  policy_digest
   117	  controller_key_digest               # = H(controller_attestation.subject_material) under policy schema
   118	  controller_attestation              # typed payload: issuer_principal_digest, subject_material,
   119	                                      #   statement_schema_id, statement_payload, signature_material
   120	  controller_attestation_digest       # = H(controller_attestation without its signature);
   121	                                      #   signature verifies over that digest; issuer must be IDENTITY_ADMIN
   122	                                      #   or TrustRoot-authorized attestor
   123	  member_principal_digests[]
   124	```
   125	
   126	**I-IND-01:** Every Principal has exactly one live atom membership per `policy_digest` used by a check (registered via Commit by IDENTITY_ADMIN). Self-membership required.  
   127	**I-IND-02:** Overlapping membership under the same policy merges into one equivalence component (transitive closure).  
   128	**I-IND-03:** Independence check for certify: atoms of proposer vs certifier are disjoint components under the policy.  
   129	**I-IND-04:** REGISTER_ATOM rejected unless attestation verifies and `controller_key_digest` matches attestation subject; all listed members’ enrollment records cite the same controller_key under the policy schema (machine-checkable enrollment field on Principal registration metadata).  
   130	**I-IND-05:** Under one `policy_digest`, all live atoms sharing the same `controller_key_digest` are one equivalence component for I-IND-02/03 (merge by controller key). REGISTER_ATOM that would introduce a second live atom with an already-live controller key under that policy is **rejected**. Membership growth uses `EXTEND_ATOM(old_atom_digest, new_atom)`: one Commit effect set that (a) retires the live atom with `old_atom_digest`, (b) installs `new_atom` with same `policy_digest` + `controller_key_digest`, recomputed `atom_digest`, and member set ⊇ old members; I-IND-04 attestation rules apply to `new_atom`. No other path may replace a live atom’s membership.
   131	
   132	Default certify checks (both required when applicable): policy `SAME_HUMAN` always; additionally `SAME_MODEL_FAMILY` when either party `kind=MODEL_RUNTIME`.
   133	
   134	---
   135	
   136	## 6. HumanDecision (constructible)
   137	
   138	```text
   139	HumanDecisionUnsigned
   140	  gate_id
   141	  target_digest
   142	  decision
   143	  principal_digest
   144	  evidence_packet_digest
   145	  expires_at_event_seq?
   146	  blast_radius_digest
   147	
   148	decision_payload_digest = H(HumanDecisionUnsigned)
   149	decision_digest = decision_payload_digest   # identity = unsigned payload; signature detached
   150	
   151	HumanDecision
   152	  decision_digest
   153	  unsigned
   154	  signature_material                  # verifies over decision_payload_digest; NOT part of decision_digest
   155	```
   156	
   157	**I-HD-AUTH-01:** Authoritative iff: Principal ACTIVE HUMAN; live HUMAN_GATE_OPERATOR binding; signature OK; gate∈ART-15; target matches gate schema; approve when authorizing; not expired at use `event_seq`; committed via authenticated Commit.  
   158	**I-HD-AUTH-02:** ESCALATE_HUMAN ≠ Decision.  
   159	**I-HD-01:** REVOKED when this artifact ACTIVE.
   160	
   161	---
   162	
   163	## 7. CertificationRecord (CERTIFIED path)
   164	
   165	```text
   166	CertificationRecordUnsigned
   167	  proof_evidence_digest               # claim DERIVED from ProofEvidence; attachment DERIVED
   168	  certifier_binding_digest            # principal + model_prov DERIVED from RoleBinding
   169	  # proposer/certifier atoms DERIVED at attachment seq via I-IND-01 for certify policies
   170	
   171	certification_digest = H(CertificationRecordUnsigned)
   172	```
   173	
   174	**I-CERTIFY-01:** Floor CERTIFIED_INFORMAL at S iff ∃ committed ATTACH_CERTIFICATION where:  
   175	- digests recompute; derived claim from evidence; derived attachment matches claim+evidence  
   176	- **ProofEvidence.kind = CERTIFIED_STRUCTURED** (LEAN_REF / INFORMAL_STRUCTURED cannot certify)  
   177	- caller binding = named `certifier_binding_digest`; derived caller principal/model_prov match binding  
   178	- derived proposer_act_event_seq’s Command created exactly this `proof_evidence_digest`; derived proposer principal/binding/model_prov match that Command (model_prov from binding)  
   179	- certifier_binding live PROOF_CERTIFIER at attachment seq  
   180	- derived atoms at attachment seq disjoint per I-IND-03 (human; model if either party MODEL_RUNTIME)  
   181	- HD valid; target_digest = certification_digest  
   182	
   183	**I-CERTIFY-02:** Else no such record ⇒ not CERTIFIED_INFORMAL (INFORMAL at best).  
   184	**I-CERTIFY-03:** Effects may add certification/proof links only — no ResearchMaturityRecord / inference axis writes.
   185	
   186	---
   187	
   188	## 8. Role × command authorization (minimum)
   189	
   190	| role_id | May submit command_kinds (non-exhaustive) |
   191	|---------|-------------------------------------------|
   192	| COMMITTER | any otherwise authorized Commit (serialization actor) |
   193	| IDENTITY_ADMIN | REGISTER/REVOKE_PRINCIPAL, BIND_ROLE (per policy), REGISTER_ATOM, EXTEND_ATOM, REGISTER_MODEL_PROV, REVOKE_MODEL_PROV (ART-11c), SEAL_RELEASE_MANIFEST (ART-25b) |
   194	| HUMAN_GATE_OPERATOR | RECORD_HUMAN_DECISION; also may embed HD in HARD_STOP_CLEAR payload |
   195	| PROOF_CERTIFIER | ATTACH_CERTIFICATION (caller must be named certifier); RECORD_CAL_SUBMECH_CERT (ART-11c) |
   196	| PROOF_PROPOSER | REGISTER_CLAIM, ATTACH_INFORMAL_PROOF, … |
   197	| EIO | SET_EIO_VETO, CLEAR_EIO_VETO, RECORD_EIO_ASSESSMENT (ART-13b); otherwise advice-only |
   198	| INTEGRATION_AUDITOR | RECORD_AUDIT (ART-11b); REGISTER_DD_VERIFICATION / RECORD_CAL_SUBMECH_CERT (ART-11c); may author N/A_UTILITY_ACK HumanDecision (ART-13b I-AP-10c) |
   199	| FRONTIER_SCHEDULER | LOCK_CYCLE (ART-08d) |
   200	| RESEARCH_ORCHESTRATOR | RECORD_DISCONFIRM (ART-11b); APPLY_PROMOTION per ART-13b; RECORD_COUNTEREXAMPLE / SET_ACTIVE_DEFINITION_HEAD / START_DEMOTION_WAVE / ADVANCE_DEMOTION_WAVE per ART-16b; SET_LEAN_TOOLCHAIN per ART-10b; BIND_CYCLE_CARD / RECORD_CYCLE_ATTACK_LOG / RECORD_CYCLE_AUDIT / RECORD_CYCLE_LEAN / ADVANCE_CYCLE / CLOSE_CYCLE per ART-08d |
   201	| LEAN_VERIFIER | RECORD_LEAN_MANIFEST (ART-10b) |
   202	
   203	Genesis HUMANS initially hold IDENTITY_ADMIN + HUMAN_GATE_OPERATOR + COMMITTER bindings.
   204

   215	
   216	```text
   217	UNKNOWN_PRINCIPAL | PRINCIPAL_REVOKED | ROLE_BINDING_MISSING | ROLE_COLLISION
   218	CMD_AUTH_FAIL | UNAUTHORIZED_COMMAND | TRUST_ROOT_VIOLATION
   219	MODEL_PROV_MISSING | MODEL_PROV_STALE | INDEPENDENCE_VIOLATION | ATOM_UNREGISTERED
   220	HD_SIGNATURE_INVALID | HD_EXPIRED | HD_GATE_MISMATCH | HD_TARGET_MISMATCH
   108	---
   109	
   110	## 5. APPLY Commit recompute
   111	
   112	Let `P` = genesis policy for `intent.policy_digest`. Let `cur` = current maturity (default `OPEN`). Let `from=P.from_value`, `to=P.to_value`.
   113	
   114	**I-AP-00 (idempotent):** If `cur=to` and latest successful APPLY for this claim has the same `intent_digest` ⇒ ACCEPTED with empty Research effects. If `cur=to` otherwise ⇒ `FROM_MISMATCH`.  
   115	**I-AP-01:** Else require `cur=from`; else `FROM_MISMATCH`.  
   116	**I-AP-02:** P registered; I-POL-02 holds; else `POLICY_MISMATCH`.  
   117	**I-AP-03:** `DerivedProofFloor(target) ≥ P.required_proof_floor` under `UNPROVED < INFORMAL < CERTIFIED_INFORMAL`; else `PROOF_FLOOR_INSUFFICIENT`.  
   118	**I-AP-05 (basis bind):**  
   119	- Each required kind’s digests resolve to that kind.  
   120	- `PROOF_ATTACHMENT`: unique valid attachment for `target_claim_digest`.  
   121	- `CERTIFICATION_RECORD`: cited digest is a CertificationRecord for which ART-04c `I-CERTIFY-01` holds **for this target using that record** at pre-state.  
   122	Else `BASIS_INVALID`.  
   123	
   124	**DerivedBasisProposers(intent, S):** for each `basis_digest` in intent:  
   125	- if CERTIFICATION_RECORD R: principal of the MutationEvent that first introduced `R`’s `proof_evidence_digest` (ART-04c I-CERTIFY-01 derived proposer).  
   126	- if PROOF_ATTACHMENT A: principal of the MutationEvent that first introduced `A.proof_evidence_digest`.  
   127	Empty basis (e.g. OPEN→CONJECTURE) ⇒ empty set (I-AR-04 vacuously true).
   128	**I-AP-06:** If any ART-07b FULL Counterexample (archived or not) has I-CX-01 hitting `intent.target_claim_digest`, require ART-16b I-DW-21: current maturity SUPERSEDED **and** COMPLETE FULL_CX DemotionWave with target ∈ seeds whose `trigger_digest` is that `cx_digest` **or** `H("CX_EXPAND", cx_digest, *)`; else `CX_BLOCKS_PROMOTION`.  
   129	**I-AP-07:** Non-noop success writes exactly one maturity upsert to `to`. No floor/ControlState/inference-endpoint writes.
   130	
   131	**I-AP-08 ValidationPreimageAPPLY.v7** (closed; no command mirrors):
   132	```text
   133	schema_id = "ART13b.ValidationPreimageAPPLY.v7"
   134	cur_maturity
   135	derived_proof_floor
   136	required_gate_ids_sorted[]
   137	basis_ok
   138	utility_ok
   139	eio_assessment_ok
   140	eio_veto_clear
   141	idempotent_noop
   142	major_milestone
   143	audit_ok                              # ART-11b: true iff non-major or I-BIND-01 holds
   144	audit_digest                          # A*.audit_digest or ⊥
   145	cycle_ok                              # ART-08d: true iff non-major or DerivedCycleMilestoneOk
   146	dd_ok                                 # ART-11c: true iff ¬data-independence/FIXED or (I-DDV-01 ∧ I-DDV-11)
   147	psi_ok                                # ART-11c: true iff ¬requires_DATA_DEP_PSI or I-PSI-02
   148	model_prov_ok                         # ART-11c: true iff no I-MP-20-stale evidence in basis
   149	roles_invoked_ok                      # ART-04d: true iff I-OM-10 RoleCeiling PASS
   150	```
   151	
   152	**EffectiveDecision(gate_id, target_digest):** latest committed HumanDecision with that `gate_id` and `target_digest` (max event_seq). Require `decision=approve` and unexpired at Commit seq; else fail. (No caller-supplied decision digests.)
   153	
   154	**I-AP-09 (veto):** If `EioVetoRecord.active=true` for target claim: EffectiveDecision(`OVERRIDE_EIO`, `H(intent_digest, veto_intro_event_seq)`) must succeed; else `EIO_VETO`.
   155	
   156	**I-AP-10 RequiredGates:**
   157	```text
   158	novelty_hit = (to > CONJECTURE) ∨ (major_milestone(claim,to) ∧ novelty_alignment(claim) ≥ PLAUSIBLE_NOVELTY)
   159	
   160	RequiredGates(claim, P, to) =
   161	  set(P.requires_gate_ids)
   162	  ∪ { PLAUSIBLE_NOVELTY_LABEL, NOVELTY_TRACK_ACK }     if novelty_hit
   163	  ∪ { INFERENCE_THEOREM_CLAIM }                         if major_milestone ∧ inference_facing(claim)
   164	  ∪ { SELECTED_OBJECT_CHANGE }                          if major_milestone ∧ policy_facing(claim)
   165	  ∪ utility_gates(claim, to)
   166	```
   167	
   168	`utility_gates`: if `to > CONJECTURE` and not `utility_compat_resolved(claim)`:  
   169	- if `stability_NA_utility(claim)` ⇒ `{N/A_UTILITY_ACK}`  
   170	- else ⇒ `{UTILITY_WAIVER}`
   171	
   172	**Closed predicates**
   173	- `major_milestone(claim,to)` — ART-01 with operational targets `{PARTIAL_RESULT, RESULT}` plus facing/novelty clauses.  
   174	- `inference_facing` — ART-01.  
   175	- `policy_facing(claim)` — `claim.chain_segment = selection_stability` ∧ `subject_ref.object_class = POLICY`.  
   176	- `novelty_alignment(claim)` — Commit-held novelty label side record; absent ⇒ below PLAUSIBLE (no novelty_hit from alignment alone).  
   177	- `utility_compat_resolved` — ∃ `UTILITY_CONSTRAINT` dep U with UtilityCompat `link_kind ∈ {PROVED_INEQUALITY, WAIVER_HUMAN}`.  
   178	- `stability_NA_utility` — `chain_segment=selection_stability` ∧ Commit-held N/A utility marker for claim.
   179	
   180	**I-AP-10b:** For every `g ∈ RequiredGates`, EffectiveDecision(`g`, `intent_digest`) must succeed; else `GATE_REQUIRED`.
   181	**I-AP-10c (N/A_UTILITY_ACK authority):** When that gate is required, the effective HD’s principal MUST have live `INTEGRATION_AUDITOR` RoleBinding at decision’s introducing seq; else `GATE_REQUIRED`.
   182	
   183	**I-AP-12 (EIO assessment — all APPLY):** Latest `EioAssessmentRecord` for `intent_digest` MUST have `outcome=ALLOW` and no later `BLOCK` for that intent; else `EIO_REQUIRED`. Combined with I-AP-09.  
   184	**I-AP-13 (demotion fence):** While ART-16b is DRAFT_REPAIR or ACTIVE_NORMATIVE: any DemotionWave with `cursor < len(work_items)` ⇒ non-noop APPLY fails `DEMOTION_WAVE_OPEN` (ART-16b I-DW-20).
   185	
   186	**I-AP-11 (audit bind):** While ART-11b is DRAFT_REPAIR or ACTIVE_NORMATIVE in this package, `major_milestone(claim,to)` ⇒ ART-11b I-BIND-01.  
   187	**I-AP-14 (cycle bind):** While ART-08d is DRAFT_REPAIR or ACTIVE_NORMATIVE, `major_milestone(claim,to)` ⇒ ART-08d DerivedCycleMilestoneOk else `CYCLE_PHASE_REQUIRED`.  
   188	**I-AP-15 (provenance bind):** While ART-11c is DRAFT_REPAIR or ACTIVE_NORMATIVE: `dd_ok` / `psi_ok` / `model_prov_ok` per ART-11c §6; else `DD_BLOCKED` | `DATA_DEP_PSI_REQUIRED` | `MODEL_PROV_STALE`. Evidence refs: ART-11c I-EV-01 else `EVIDENCE_PROV_FAIL`.  
   189	**I-AP-16 (role ceiling):** While ART-04d is DRAFT_REPAIR or ACTIVE_NORMATIVE: major APPLY ⇒ ART-04d I-OM-10 else `ROLE_CEILING`.
   190	
   191	**I-BOOL-02:** Maturity axis writes only via APPLY_PROMOTION; ART-16b `ADVANCE_DEMOTION_WAVE`; or ART-16b I-DW-30/33 seed SUPERSEDE on `RECORD_COUNTEREXAMPLE` (FULL) / `START_DEMOTION_WAVE` / I-DW-33; else `AXIS_WRITE_FORBIDDEN`.

    54

    59	## 2. Closure + I-DDV-20 (Commit-executable)
    60	
    61	**Declared closure** of a `DataDependenceRecord` = the `reads[]` array in `derivation_payload` (canonical JSON). No other ambient walk.
    62	
    63	**I-DDV-20** (`REGISTER_DD_VERIFICATION` checks; all required):
    64	1. `dd_digest` resolves to live DataDependenceRecord D.
    65	2. Schema of `D.derivation_schema_id` is registered.
    66	3. Every `reads[].kind` ∈ schema `allowed_read_kinds` (FIXED ⇒ must be empty list and `reads[]` empty).
    67	4. If schema `dd_class_fixed=true`: `reads=[]` else reject `DD_HIDDEN_READ` when any read present; `closure_digest` MUST equal `H("EMPTY")`.
    68	5. If not FIXED: `closure_digest = H(sorted (kind,digest) pairs of reads)` MUST equal payload; empty reads allowed only if schema permits.
    69	6. `status=VERIFIED` only if 1–5 hold; `REJECTED` always allowed for auditor judgment (incl. suspected undeclared reads).
    70	
    71	**Schema marker:** ART-07b `SchemaRegistryEntry` for a FIXED derivation schema MUST set `dd_class_fixed=true` and `allowed_read_kinds=[]` (empty). Non-FIXED schemas list allowed kinds ⊆ {DATASET,CALIBRATION,ADAPTIVE,EXTERNAL_FETCH,OTHER}.

   111	## 6. I-BIND-01
   112	
   113	Non-noop APPLY only (I-AP-00 skips).  
   114	Let `A*` = AuditRecord with `intent_digest=intent_digest(PI)` and **maximum introducing event_seq** (any verdict). Require `A*.verdict=PASS`; if latest is FAIL/IRRELEVANT/ESCALATE_HUMAN or missing ⇒ `AUDIT_REQUIRED` / `AUDIT_FAIL` (a later non-PASS supersedes prior PASS).  
   115	Freshness since intro(A*): no later Effects upserting ResearchMaturityRecord[target]; ProofAttachment/CertificationRecord/DisconfirmLog for target; EioVeto[target]; EioAssessment[intent_digest(PI)]; Claim[target]; bridge Claim = I-INF BRIDGE dep of target; UTILITY Claim cited by target; Discharge for target; Mechanism of target; **or** (at APPLY, under **current** ClaimRelation/fingerprint state) any **live** Counterexample whose ART-07b I-CX-01 closure hits any digest in EvidenceClosure(A*, intent). The Counterexample clause is not limited to Counterexamples introduced after intro(A*): a pre-existing FULL Counterexample that becomes connected to audited evidence via a later RENAMES/EQUIVALENT_TO edge still stales the audit when I-CX-01 is re-evaluated at APPLY.
   116	
   117	**EvidenceClosure(A*, intent)** (Claim digests only — I-CX-01 does not hit object-table digests):
   118	1. `intent.target_claim_digest`
   119	2. every digest in answers’ `evidence_digests[]` whose EvidenceKindResolver kind is CLAIM, UTILITY, or BRIDGE (Claim digests)
   120	3. for each answer digest of kind PROOF: if ProofAttachment, use that attachment’s `claim_digest` and the linked PE’s `lemma_claim_digests[]`; if ProofEvidence, use `PE.claim_digest` ∪ `PE.lemma_claim_digests[]`
   121	4. for each answer digest of kind CERT (CERT table / CertificationRecord): resolve `CertificationRecord.proof_evidence_digest` → PE; include `PE.claim_digest` ∪ `PE.lemma_claim_digests[]`
   122	5. for each answer digest of kind DISCHARGE: `DischargeRecord.discharger_claim_digest` ∪ `DischargeRecord.target_claim_digest`; if a ProofAttachment exists for `discharger_claim_digest`, also union that PE’s `lemma_claim_digests[]` (same depth as PROOF path)
   123	
   124	Do **not** put `proof_evidence_digest` / `certification_digest` / `attachment_digest` / `discharge_digest` themselves into EvidenceClosure for CX freshness (wrong type for I-CX-01).
   125	
   126	Re-hold I-AR-03…05 with intent=PI; ValidationPreimageAPPLY.v7 audit_ok + audit_digest.
   127	DRAFT or ACTIVE.

    68	
    69	**I-CY-01..03:** quarantine frozen; ≤1 open cycle; cycle_digest immutable.
    70	
    71	Genesis **MandatoryAttackClass[quarantine.class] → set of class_id** (closed package set).  
    72	Genesis **ClassConstructSchema[class_id] → construct_schema_id** (closed; every mandatory class_id has a row).
    73	
    74	---
    75	
    76	## 2. Derived predicates
    77	
    78	**DerivedHopChainOk(C):**  
    79	- `example_card_digest` set; ExampleCard.quarantine_digest=C.quarantine_digest; `chain_link_intent = QuarantineLock.chain_link`  
    80	- `falsifier_card_digest` set; FalsifierCard.quarantine_digest=C.quarantine_digest; `chain_segment = QuarantineLock.chain_link = ExampleCard.chain_link_intent`
    81	
    82	**DerivedS09Ok(C):** AttackLog.cycle_digest=C.cycle_digest; let R=MandatoryAttackClass[C.quarantine.class];  
    83	∀ class_id ∈ R: ∃ unique attempt with that class_id;  
    84	∀ attempt: live Counterexample CX at cx_digest such that  
    85	1. I-CX-01 hits `C.target_claim_digest`, and  
    86	2. `CX.construct_schema_id = ClassConstructSchema[class_id].construct_schema_id`, and  
    87	3. ART-07b **I-SCH-01 SchemaValid**(`ClassConstructSchema[class_id].construct_schema_id`, `CX.construct_payload`) holds  
    88	else false. Unknown class_id ⇒ `UNKNOWN_ATTACK_CLASS`.
    89	**DerivedS10Ok(C):** AuditRecord present; major PASS path ⇒ verdict=PASS ∧ I-BIND-01 for intent targeting `C.target_claim_digest`.
    90	
    91	**IncompleteWave:** DemotionWave `cursor < len(work_items)`.
    92	
    93	**DerivedMathStable(C):** DerivedHopChainOk ∧ DefPins match ∧ ¬IncompleteWave ∧ I-DW-21 ok for target ∧ (lean set ⇒ ≠ LEAN_STALE).
    94	
    95	**DerivedCycleMilestoneOk(C, intent):** intent.target=`C.target_claim_digest`; S09+S10+hop+MathStable; phase≥S10; audit PASS.
    96	
    97	---

    99	## 3. Commands
   100	
   101	**LOCK_CYCLE** (FRONTIER_SCHEDULER): `{ QuarantineLock, primary_question_digest, target_claim_digest }` → phase=S02.
   102	
   103	**BIND_CYCLE_CARD** (RESEARCH_ORCHESTRATOR): `{ cycle_digest, ExampleCard | FalsifierCard }`

   105	- FalsifierCard only at phase=S03 and `falsifier_card_digest` currently ⊥ else `CARD_FROZEN`

   107

   109

   119	S04→S03
   120	S03→S05

   124	S07→S08 | S07→S09

    58	## 2. DerivedLeanStatus
    59	
    60	**Rank:** LEAN_FULL=5, LEAN_CORE=4, LEAN_STATEMENT=3, LEAN_BLOCKED=2, NOT_READY_FOR_LEAN=1, LEAN_STALE=0.
    61	
    62	Latest LeanManifest for C; none ⇒ `NOT_READY_FOR_LEAN`.
    63	
    64	**I-LM-10:** If pin≠head ∨ toolchain≠head ∨ FULL CX hits C ∨ SUPERSEDED ⇒ `LEAN_STALE`.  
    65	Else if `build_ok=false` ⇒ `LEAN_BLOCKED`.  
    66	Else if sorry=0 ∧ admit=0 ∧ no custom axiom asserts target ⇒ `LEAN_FULL`.  
    67	Else if build_ok ∧ sorry=0 ∧ admit=0 ⇒ `LEAN_CORE`.  
    68	Else if build_ok ⇒ `LEAN_STATEMENT`.  
    69	Else `NOT_READY_FOR_LEAN`.

    79	ActiveDefinitionHead                  # mutable via ART-06b I.Commit only
    80	  def_id
    81	  active_def_version_digest
    82	  set_at_event_id

    84

    92	
    93	**I-DEF-01:** Identifier nodes are `{def_id, symbol}`; pins must resolve; inventory = fixed walk over normative math fields.
    94	
    95	**I-DEF-02:** Non-historical promotion requires ActiveDefinitionHead match. Historical scope uses side binding (not inside `claim_digest`):
    96	
    97	```text
    98	HistoricalScopeBinding                # side table; NOT hashed into claim_digest
    99	  binding_digest                      # = H(claim_digest, historical_scope_dec_digest)
   100	  claim_digest
   101	  historical_scope_dec_digest
   102	```
   103

Valid iff ART-04c I-HD-AUTH allows authoritative decisions **and** HumanDecision has `gate_id=HISTORICAL_SCOPE_ACK`, `decision=approve`, `target_digest=claim_digest` when historical_scoped.
   105	
   106	**I-DEF-03:** SchemaRegistry admits a schema only if a **structural** check (recursive walk) finds no unconstrained string node carrying mathematical meaning. `forbids_opaque_string_roots` is a derived bit, not an author boolean. Allowed strings: citations/labels/render keys with `explanatory: true`.

   110	## 3. Assumption and discharge
   111	
   112	```text
   113	Assumption
   114	  assumption_digest
   115	  statement_schema_id                 # schema_role=PROPOSITION
   116	  statement_payload
   117	  kind                                # AXIOM | HYPOTHESIS | MODELING | DATA | GATE_WAIVER
   118	  prerequisite_assumption_digests[]
   119	  definition_pins[]
   120	```
   121	
   122	```text
   123	AssumptionUse
   124	  slot_id
   125	  assumption_digest
   126	  role                                # REQUIRED | DISCHARGED
   127	```
   128	
   129	```text
   130	DischargeRecord                       # side table; NEVER in claim_digest
   131	  discharge_digest                    # = H(target_claim_digest, slot_id, assumption_digest, discharger_claim_digest)
   132	  target_claim_digest
   133	  slot_id
   134	  assumption_digest
   135	  discharger_claim_digest
   136	```
   137	
   138	**I-ASM-01 (computable REQUIRED closure):**  
   139	Let `StmtDigest(A) = H(A.statement_schema_id, A.statement_payload)`.  
   140	`Prereq*(a) = {a} ∪ ⋃_{p ∈ Assumption(a).prerequisite_assumption_digests} Prereq*(p)` (must be finite/acyclic; cycles ⇒ invalid Assumption).  
   141	
   142	For Claim C:  
   143	`DirectRequired(C) = ⋃_{u ∈ C.assumptions_used, u.role=REQUIRED} Prereq*(u.assumption_digest)`  
   144	`InheritedRequired(C) = ⋃_{d ∈ C.dependencies} NetRequired(premise(d))`  
   145	`GrossRequired(C) = DirectRequired(C) ∪ InheritedRequired(C)`  
   146	
   147	A DischargeRecord r is applicable to C iff r.target_claim_digest=C.claim_digest and r valid per I-ASM-02 and there exists AssumptionUse u on C with u.slot_id=r.slot_id, u.assumption_digest=r.assumption_digest, u.role=DISCHARGED.  
   148	
   149	`NetRequired(C) = GrossRequired(C) \ { r.assumption_digest | r applicable to C }`  
   150	`NetRequiredDigest(C) = H(sorted unique digests in NetRequired(C))`  
   250	### 4.3 Chain segments (complete front-to-back)
   251	
   252	| chain_segment | Required bindings |
   253	|---------------|-------------------|
   254	| `data_regime` | typed data/neighbor regime |
   255	| `score_construction` | exactly one LOGICAL dep on `data_regime`; predecessor from dep; scope+DefPins equal; payload includes `score_family_digest = H(score_family_schema_id, score_family_payload)` |
   256	| `perturbation_law` | exactly one LOGICAL dep on `score_construction`; `score_family_digest` MUST equal predecessor.score_family_digest; mechanism_digest; operator; parameter_domain_digest = mechanism.parameter_domain_digest; scope+DefPins equal predecessor |
   257	| `selection_application` | exactly one LOGICAL dep on `perturbation_law`; `score_family_digest` = predecessor.score_family_digest; subject_ref.subject_digest = ApplyOperatorDigest(operator, mechanism, score_family_digest) where ApplyOperatorDigest is the registered schema projection for that operator; mechanism/domain/scope/DefPins equal predecessor |
   258	| `selection_stability` | exactly one LOGICAL dep on `selection_application`; subject/mechanism/domain/scope/DefPins/score_family_digest equal predecessor; guarantee_kind + failure_budget_digest required |
   259	| `perturbation` | optional dep on `perturbation_law`; guarantee_kind required; certificate only if in `certificate_segments` |
   260	| `composition` | ≥1 input: at least one LOGICAL dep on a certificate-segment Claim; ordered deps; `composition_rule_digest`; EvidenceUse(COMPOSITION_INPUT) DERIVED; rule schema defines output subject/mechanism/domain/scope/DefPins/failure_budget/guarantee_core/score_family from inputs — all must validate |
   261	| `selected_object` | exactly one LOGICAL dep on generator ∈ {composition, selection_stability}; subject/mechanism/domain/scope/DefPins equal generator; covering_cert DERIVED = generator |
   262	| `bridge` | see I-BRIDGE-01 |
   263	| `inference` | exactly one BRIDGE dep; exactly one LOGICAL dep on `selected_object`; subject = selected_object.subject = source cert subject; inference_target; failure_budget_digest present |
   264	
   265	**I-CHAIN-01:** Missing prior-segment dependency or failed digest-equality projection ⇒ invalid.
   266	
   267	**I-CERT-01:** Stability/perturbation: payload guarantee fields equal Claim-level mechanism, domain, subject; MechanismInstance operator+domain match; shared DefPins agree; `guarantee_kind` ∈ `{NEIGHBOR_INDISTINGUISHABILITY, ORACLE_COMPARISON, OTHER_TYPED}`.
   268	
   269	**I-COMP-01:** `composition_rule_digest` → CertificateCompositionRule (schema_role=COMPOSITION_RULE), distinct from SelectionOperator.  
   270	Output endpoint completeness: ART-07c **I-COMP-ENDPOINT-01**.
   271	
   272	**I-COMP-02:** ≥1 input cert; each input segment ∈ `certificate_segments` (default `{selection_stability, composition}`). Zero-input composition invalid.
   273	
   274	**I-OBJ-SEL-01:** selected_object required on inference path (no direct generator skip).
   275	
   276	**I-BRIDGE-01:** Inference Claims: unique BRIDGE premise = bridge Claim digest; covering anchor `source_cert_claim_digest = selected_object.covering_cert`; `subject_ref.subject_digest` equals source cert subject.  
   277	**Inference validity beyond covering/subject (endpoint equality + APPLICABLE evaluator):** ART-07c **I-INF-CLAIM-01** (authoritative).  
   278	**All certificate/bridge endpoint typing, transforms, floors, and composition:** ART-07c.
   279	
   280	**I-INF-01:** inference_target is schema_role=INFERENCE_TARGET; never aliased to subject_digest.
   281	
   282	```text
   283	CertificateCompositionRule
   284	  rule_digest
   285	  rule_schema_id
   286	  rule_payload
   287	  definition_pins[]
   288	```
   289	
   290	### 4.3a Embedded transforms + TransformRule
   291	
   292	```text
   293	TransformRecord                       # embedded in bridge payload — ART-07c normative carrier
   294	  transform_kind                      # see ART-07c §7 (includes ENDPOINT_CONSTRUCTION)
   295	  input_digest
   296	  output_digest
   297	  rule_payload
   298	  # proof-bound kinds: LOGICAL premise of bridge with matching TransformApplicationDigest (ART-07c)
   299	```
   300	
   301	**I-XFORM-01:** Digests recompute; kind + proof binding per ART-07c §7.  
   302	Legacy standalone `TransformRule` / `rule_digest` / `rule_proof_claim_digest` fields are **non-authoritative**.
   303	
   304	### 4.3b EvidenceUse
   305	
   306	```text
   307	EvidenceUse
   308	  evidence_use_digest                 # = H(consumer, evidence, mediation, slot_id, bridge?)
   309	  consumer_claim_digest
   310	  evidence_claim_digest
   311	  mediation                           # DIRECT | BRIDGE_TRANSFORM | COMPOSITION_INPUT
   312	  slot_id                             # unique per consumer citation slot
   313	  bridge_claim_digest?                # required for BRIDGE_TRANSFORM
   314	```
   315	
   316	**I-CERT-02:** At most one EvidenceUse per `(consumer, slot_id)`. Conflicting mediations for same slot invalid.
   317	
   318	**I-CERT-02a DIRECT:** equal subject/mechanism/domain/scope/DefPins/guarantee_core; segment pair allowed; schema accepted.
   319	
   320	**I-CERT-02b BRIDGE_TRANSFORM:** bridge.valid; source_cert=evidence; transforms OK; subjects digest-exact;  
   321	consumer endpoint + APPLICABLE per ART-07c **I-CERT-TRANSFER-01** (certificate transfer) or **I-INF-CLAIM-01** (inference).
   322	
   323	**I-CERT-02c COMPOSITION_INPUT:** DERIVED from ordered LOGICAL deps of composition Claim; slot_id = dependency index; subjects need not match across inputs.
   324	
   325	### 4.4 Data dependence (acyclic)
   326	
   327	```text
   328	parameter_domain_core = (domain_schema_id, domain_payload_without_dd)
   329	parameter_domain_core_digest = H(parameter_domain_core)
   330	
   331	DataDependenceRecord                  # may be embedded in domain or content-addressed
   332	# DdVerificationRecord — ART-11c (authoritative in ART-11c §1)
   333	  dd_digest                           # = H(derivation_schema_id, derivation_payload, parameter_domain_core_digest)
   334	  derivation_schema_id
   335	  derivation_payload
   336	  bound_core_digest                   # MUST equal parameter_domain_core_digest
   337	  class                               # DERIVED
   338	
   339	parameter_domain_digest = H(parameter_domain_core_digest, dd_digest)
   340	```
   341	
   342	**I-DD-01:** class derived; FIXED only via registered FIXED derivation with no dataset/calibration/adaptive refs.  
   343	**I-DD-02:** bound_core_digest equality required; no circular hash of full domain into DD.  
   344	**I-DD-03:** promotion floors recompute class from dd_digest.
   345

     4	**Version:** `ARCH-0.3-REPAIR-ITER2.7`

     9

    11	
    12	Exact typed, digest-native certificates and Bridge Theorems. No label interchange. No CompatibilityPolicy. Certificate = ART-07b Claim. No parallel cert/bridge lifecycle axes — floors + evaluator results only.
    13	
    14	## Scope lock
    15	
    16	Fixed research chain only. Neighbor / oracle / DP / robustness / utility / coverage / generalization / policy validity are never interchangeable without an explicit Bridge Theorem.

    19	
    20	## 1. Theorem certificate
    21	
    22	A theorem-level stability certificate **is** an ART-07b `Claim` with `chain_segment ∈ certificate_segments`.
    23	
    24	**I-CERT-EMP-01:** Empirical-only evidence or `research_maturity=CONJECTURE` without theorem ProofAttachment MUST NOT occupy theorem certificate endpoints or inference-facing bridge sources.
    25	
    26	No `StabilityCertificateInstance`, no author `CertificateEpistemicClass`, no bridge lifecycle enum.
    27

    30	## 2. ComparisonRelation
    31	
    32	```text
    33	ComparisonRelation
    34	  relation_digest                     # = H(normative fields)
    35	  relation_schema_id                  # schema_role=COMPARISON_RELATION
    36	  relation_kind                       # REPLACE_ONE_NEIGHBOR | ADD_REMOVE_ONE_NEIGHBOR
    37	                                      # | BOUNDED_OBS_PERTURB | ORACLE_COMPARISON
    38	                                      # | SCORE_VECTOR_PERTURB | PARAMETER_PERTURB
    39	                                      # | CANDIDATE_SET_PERTURB | DISTRIBUTIONAL_COMPARISON
    40	                                      # | ALGORITHM_RANDOMNESS_COMPARISON | OTHER_TYPED
    41	  domain_schema_id / domain_payload
    42	  directionality                      # SYMMETRIC | DIRECTED
    43	  adjacency_or_metric
    44	  radius_or_budget?                   # required when kind has radius
    45	  quantified_objects[]
    46	  exclusions[]
    47	  definition_pins[]
    48	```
    49	
    50	**I-CERT-RELATION-01:** Each certificate Claim binds exactly one `relation_digest`.

    55
